Write each file using the Write tool.\n\nThe configuration uses:\n- `define TEC_RV_ICG clockhdr` (clock gating cell)\n- `define USER_EC_RV_ICG user_clock_gate` (user clock gate in testbench)\n- DCCM: 4 banks, 4096 rows, 39-bit data (32+7 ECC), ram_4096x39\n- ICCM: 4 banks, 4096 rows, 39-bit data (32+7 ECC), ram_4096x39\n- ICache data: ram_512x71 (71 bits with ECC), 2 ways, 2 banks\n- ICache tag: ram_128x25, 2 ways\n\nFILE 1: /app/design/lib/beh_lib.sv\nThis is the behavioral library containing memory macro models. 32'h03FC07F0);\n    ecc[4] = ^(data & 32'h03FFF800);\n    ecc[5] = ^(data & 32'h7FC00000);\n    ecc[6] = ^(data & 32'hFFFFFFFF);\n    return ecc;\nendfunction\n\nfunction automatic [31:0] el2_ecc_decode(input [38:0] data_ecc);\n    logic [6:0] ecc_computed;\n    logic [6:0] ecc_syndrome;\n    logic [31:0] data;\n    data = data_ecc[31:0];\n    ecc_computed = el2_ecc_encode(data);\n    ecc_syndrome = ecc_computed ^ data_ecc[38:32];\n    if (|ecc_syndrome) begin\n        case (ecc_syndrome)\n            7'b0000001, 7'b0000010, 7'b0000100, 7'b0001000,\n            7'b0010000, 7'b0100000, 7'b1000000: begin\n            end\n            default: begin\n                data[ecc_syndrome[2:0]] = ~data[ecc_syndrome[2:0]];\n            end\n        endcase\n    end\n    return data;\nendfunction\n\nendpackage\n\nmodule el2_ecc_encode\n(\n   input  logic [31:0] din,\n   output logic [38:0] ecc_out\n);\n   logic [6:0] ecc;\n   assign ecc[0] = ^(din & 32'h56AAAD5B);\n   assign ecc[1] = ^(din & 32'h9B333666);\n   assign ecc[2] = ^(din & 32'hE3C3C78E);\n   assign ecc[3] = ^(din & 32'h03FC07F0);\n   assign ecc[4] = ^(din & 32'h03FFF800);\n   assign ecc[5] = ^(din & 32'h7FC00000);\n   assign ecc[6] = ^(din);\n   assign ecc_out = {ecc, din};\nendmodule\n\nmodule el2_ecc_decode\n(\n   input  logic [38:0] ecc_in,\n   output logic [31:0] dout,\n   output logic        single_ecc_error,\n   output logic        double_ecc_error\n);\n   logic [6:0] ecc_syndrome;\n   logic [6:0] ecc_computed;\n   logic [31:0] data;\n   assign data = ecc_in[31:0];\n   assign 32'h03FC07F0);\n   assign ecc_computed[4] = ^(data & 32'h03FFF800);\n   assign ecc_computed[5] = ^(data & 32'h7FC00000);\n   assign ecc_computed[6] = ^(data);\n   assign ecc_syndrome = ecc_computed ^ ecc_in[38:32];\n   assign single_ecc_error = |ecc_syndrome;\n   assign double_ecc_error = ecc_syndrome[6];\n   logic [31:0] corrected_data;\n   always_comb begin\n      corrected_data = data;\n
